# Graph Report - fastapi  (2026-09-26)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 3141 files · ~2,006,530 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 29040 nodes · 36806 edges · 2593 communities (2005 shown, 588 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 437 edges (avg confidence: 0.89)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output
